- **per-cell-regulator-activity-comparison-across-clusters**: Use when you have a fitted scKINETICS model (GRN matrix A + per-cell velocities) and need TF-level, per-cell activity scores to identify regulators whose influence varies across clusters, e.g. acinar vs transitioning vs duct-like populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cell-regulator-activity-comparison-across-clusters))
- **per-cell-signature-score-visualization**: Use when per-cell annotation or disease scores (e.g., LHSC_per_cell_score, Neoplastic_monocyte_per_cell_score) are stored in a Seurat object's metadata and you need to compare their spatial/UMAP distributions across biological conditions such as healthy vs AML or diagnosis vs relapse. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cell-signature-score-visualization))
- **per-cell-snv-genotyping-matrix-construction**: Use when you have long-read (ONT/PacBio) amplicon reads from 10x Genomics 5' scRNA-seq cDNA libraries targeting known somatic mutations and need per-cell genotype calls and genotyping rates keyed by cell barcode. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-cell-snv-genotyping-matrix-construction))
- **per-cell-tei-computation**: Use when an AnnData scRNA-seq object and a gene-age orthomap (e.g. from OrthoFinder/oggmap) are available and you need gene age-weighted expression summaries such as per-cell or per-stage TEI to test developmental hourglass patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cell-tei-computation))
- **per-cell-type-accuracy-heatmap-visualization**: Use when you have cell-type label predictions for query datasets mapped onto a pre-trained reference (e.g., scCobra KNN label transfer on GSE114297/GSE85247/GSE83312 pancreas queries) and need a per-cell-type accuracy breakdown, such as reproducing the Fig. 6b-e ACC heatmap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cell-type-accuracy-heatmap-visualization))
- **per-cell-type-auroc-evaluation**: Use when you have a pretrained Miscell latent feature matrix (e.g. from PBMC scRNA-seq) with known cell-type labels and need to quantify how well those features separate 7 cell types via a linear probe. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cell-type-auroc-evaluation))
- **per-cell-type-confidence-aggregation**: Use when comparing cell-type annotation quality between two representations of Visium HD data (e.g. bin2cell cells vs 8 μm bins) and you need per-cell-type tallies of median confidence and mean genes detected. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-cell-type-confidence-aggregation))
- **per-cell-type-density-estimation**: Use when scRNA-seq cells carry per-cell activity scores (from ORIGINS2 activity()) plus known cell-type labels, and you need density distributions per type to compare biological programs across clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cell-type-density-estimation))
- **per-cell-type-doublet-proportion-quantification**: Use when Chord (or another doublet caller) has output predicted doublet barcodes for annotated scRNA-seq samples and you need per-sample doublet rates and per-cell-type doublet proportions to identify doublet-enriched populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cell-type-doublet-proportion-quantification))
- **per-cell-type-isoform-quantification**: Use when you have PacBio/ONT single-cell long-read fastq.gz files plus a barcode-to-cell-type assignment table (e.g. from Seurat short-read analysis) and need per-cell-type isoform, TSS, or polyA counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cell-type-isoform-quantification))
- **per-cell-type-mean-expression-computation**: Use when you have a labeled scRNA-seq reference matrix (cells × genes with cell-type annotations) and need cell-type-level mean expression vectors to feed the E-test ΔS gene-selection statistic or a multinomial MLE classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cell-type-mean-expression-computation))
- **per-cell-type-scaled-expression-differential-analysis**: Use when a gene metagene or embedding-derived gene program must be validated as cell-type-independent by comparing per-cell scaled expression of its member genes between conditions (e.g., stimulated vs control) within each annotated cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cell-type-scaled-expression-differential-analysis))
- **per-cell-umi-count-statistical-testing**: Use when two 10x scRNA-seq libraries must be compared for equal per-cell capture depth (genes/UMIs per cell), e.g. fresh vs cryopreserved PBMCs, after matching mean reads per cell by downsampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cell-umi-count-statistical-testing))
- **per-cell-viral-read-count-aggregation**: Use when you have per-sample pathogen_al_counts.csv files from scPathoQuant runs over a multi-virus reference (e.g. SARS-CoV-2 + metapneumovirus) and need to aggregate per-cell counts into a per-virus infected-cell and read-abundance table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cell-viral-read-count-aggregation))
- **per-challenge-failure-taxonomy**: 1. Every challenge must have **all 4 tiers populated** 2. Each tier must predict: primary archetype, score range, specific behavior, what they'll miss 3. Score ranges must **not overlap more than 10 points** between adjacent tiers 4. Archetypes must reference the **standard 15** (Skill 48) 5. Predicted behaviors must be **specific to this challenge** — not generic descriptions 6. "What they'll mis ([nickgallick/perlantir-fleet](https://github.com/nickgallick/perlantir-fleet/tree/HEAD/workspace-gauntlet/skills/per-challenge-failure-taxonomy))
- **per-chat-config**: Add a per-chat configurable knob (spam_threshold, report_hour, captcha_difficulty) — migration → ChatConfig struct → API DTO → transactional update endpoint. ([PlugFox/vixen-rs](https://github.com/PlugFox/vixen-rs/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/server/per-chat-config) · ★3)
- **per-chromosome-genomic-array-io**: Use when a pipeline needs to persist or reload per-chromosome, binned genomic annotation arrays (e.g. deepC skeleton tags in 1–10 range) from Hi-C contact maps at 5 kb resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/per-chromosome-genomic-array-io))
- **per-chromosome-phased-assembly**: Use when you have a long-read WGS BAM (PacBio HiFi/CLR or ONT) from a diploid sample and need a phased, per-chromosome diploid assembly as input for SV calling or resource benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-chromosome-phased-assembly))
- **per-class-bgc-similarity-tuning**: Use when tuning BiG-SCAPE pairwise distance weights per BGC class, e.g. when JI/AI/DSS combinations must be optimized against MIBiG compound structural distances for classes like RiPPs or PKS/NRP hybrids. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-class-bgc-similarity-tuning))
- **per-class-f1-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking an annotation or classification model whose improvement is concentrated in minority classes, and aggregate metrics would hide the rare-class gains. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-class-f1-benchmark-evaluation))
- **per-class-f1-score-computation**: Use when you have predicted cell-type annotations from a tool like Census plus reference labels for the same cells and need per-class F1 scores, or need to test annotation robustness across embedding parameter grids. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-class-f1-score-computation))
- **per-class-f1-score-evaluation**: Use when a galaxy/QSO/star classifier (e.g. SHEEP's XGBoost/LightGBM/CatBoost ensemble) has been trained on SDSS DR15+WISE photometry and you need per-class F1-scores to validate against the reported values or to run baseline/ablation comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/per-class-f1-score-evaluation))
- **per-class-f1-score-evaluation**: Use when a multiclass or hierarchical cell-type classifier must be validated against labelled ground truth and reported per cell type, e.g. fivefold cross-validation on the pan-cancer training reference or the blood/stroma/cancer benchmark of Fig. 2c. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-class-f1-score-evaluation))
- **per-class-precision-recall-f1-computation**: Use when you have a multiclass confusion matrix or class-level prediction counts and need to identify which class labels (e.g. NPClassifier superclasses) a model like DeepSAT predicts worst. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-class-precision-recall-f1-computation))
- **per-class-recall-performance-benchmarking**: Use when comparing classifiers trained on different gene feature spaces over the five PAM50 subtypes, and you need per-class (subtype) recalls, macro-averaged recall, and precision on a held-out test split rather than aggregate accuracy alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-class-recall-performance-benchmarking))
- **per-class-sensitivity-specificity-evaluation**: Use when a CMS prediction (or any 4-class sample-subtype assignment) must be validated sample-by-sample against original reference labels on a held-out cohort and per-class sensitivity/specificity are required, not just a single accuracy figure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-class-sensitivity-specificity-evaluation))
- **per-client-memory-router**: Stops one agent working N clients from bleeding context between them, and stops N parallel client jobs from starving each other on the same model quota. Partition memory into a global store plus one isolated store per client, then gate execution through a concurrency-limited router so no single client's job queue can starve the rest. Use when one agentic setup serves multiple clients, when a client's context/preferences ever showed up in another client's output, or when parallel client jobs are timing out or burning quota unevenly. ([search-atlas-group/amm-founding-circle](https://github.com/search-atlas-group/amm-founding-circle/tree/HEAD/skills/per-client-memory-router))
- **per-clip-scene-research**: Use when planning or generating any film clip: research similar cinema stills in the browser first, analyse the setup, then write the card. Never generate from adjectives or memory alone. ([Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates](https://github.com/Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates/tree/HEAD/templates/community/content-publishing/best-video-editor/skills/per-clip-scene-research) · ★6)
- **per-cluster-consensus-cnv-imputation**: Use when per-cell CNV calls from scATAC-seq data are noisy or sparse and cluster-level consensus genotypes are needed — e.g. after identifyCNVClusters has produced a CNV cluster file and Louvain cell clusters exist. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/per-cluster-consensus-cnv-imputation))
- **per-cluster-interaction-count-aggregation**: Use when you have pre-computed CCI datasets (e.g., CellPhoneDB output) for two or more conditions and want to identify cell clusters whose total interaction counts differ strikingly between conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cluster-interaction-count-aggregation))
- **per-cluster-marker-ranking-by-ncdi**: Use when analyzing a preprocessed Seurat object (e.g., Tyser 2021, 1195 cells) and the goal is cluster-specific binary markers — genes expressed exclusively in one cell population — rather than graded average-expression DE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cluster-marker-ranking-by-ncdi))
- **per-cluster-metric-aggregation**: Use when a per-cell metric must be summarized per annotated cluster and globally to compare methods, e.g. local velocity inconsistency via mean cosine distance to k=30 neighbors across velocity methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cluster-metric-aggregation--5d95bdbf40826eb9))
- **per-cluster-metric-aggregation**: Use when mapping a query single-cell dataset onto a Symphony reference and you need to detect query cell populations (clusters) that are absent from the reference, e.g. after holding out or ablating a cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cluster-metric-aggregation--9782ab8acb0fa386))
- **per-cluster-score-aggregation-and-thresholding**: Use when a cell-by-cell-type score matrix (e.g. ScType es.max output on a clustered Seurat object) must be collapsed into one label per cluster, with a minimum-score threshold to flag unannotated clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-cluster-score-aggregation-and-thresholding))
- **per-cohort-z-score-scaling**: Use when a matrix of Nightingale NMR metabolic biomarker concentrations contains below-detection-limit zeroes and must be log-transformed and z-scaled within each study before cohort-level survival or meta-analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-cohort-z-score-scaling))
- **per-component-concentration-position-refinement**: Use when an initial CRNN-based spectral decomposition of 1H NMR plasma spectra leaves residual error and individual metabolite components need concentration scaling and peak-position adjustment to lower the per-metabolite reconstruction MSE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-component-concentration-position-refinement))
- **per-component-versioning**: Use when working with releases, version bumps, semantic-release, component tags, Renovate scopes, or adding a new reusable workflow/action component to isw-kudos/github-actions. Triggers include questions like "how is X released", "what version bump will this commit produce", "how do I add a new component", "why didn't a release fire", or any change to release-*.yml, releases/**, or renovate.json packageRules. ([isw-kudos/github-actions](https://github.com/isw-kudos/github-actions/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/per-component-versioning))
- **per-compound-majority-vote-aggregation**: Use when a compound-level decision must be derived from multiple per-spectrum classifier outputs (e.g. EI-MS and ESI-MS/MS spectra of the same compound) and a single bitter/nonbitter label is required for precision/recall evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-compound-majority-vote-aggregation))
- **per-compound-match-table-compilation**: Use when you have parsed MASST match records for a library of synthesized compounds and need to compile per-compound counts of matched public files and total matches, then reconcile totals against reported figures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-compound-match-table-compilation))
- **per-compound-spectrum-merging**: Use when training spectra-presence predictors from multiple spectra per compound and each compound needs one canonical binary target spectrum rather than intensity-level prediction across varying collision energies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-compound-spectrum-merging))
- **per-concept-documentation**: Required when reviewing, updating, auditing, or creating any concept documentation page. Covers code accuracy checks against source repos, language parity across zone pivots, parity table comments, and structural consistency. ([MicrosoftDocs/semantic-kernel-docs](https://github.com/MicrosoftDocs/semantic-kernel-docs/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/per-concept-documentation) · ★264)
- **per-condition-summary-statistics-computation**: Use when a MaxQuant peptides.txt table contains replicate intensity columns named 'Intensity &lt;condition&gt;x' (e.g. HSILx, ecto Cx, endo Cx) and per-condition average intensity and positive-replicate counts must be appended for downstream MALDImID peak identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/per-condition-summary-statistics-computation))
- **per-configuration-result-tabulation**: Use when multiple segmentation configurations (e.g., loss-weight ablations) have been run and per-run metric values must be collected, tabulated, and compared against reference trends. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-configuration-result-tabulation))
- **per-content-type-confidence-threshold-dispatch**: Apply per-content-type confidence thresholds (read from config) and a 3-mode dispatch (best_guess / medium_confidence / high_confidence) to gate model predictions vs generic fallbacks. ([kjuhwa/skills-hub](https://github.com/kjuhwa/skills-hub/tree/HEAD/skills/inference/per-content-type-confidence-threshold-dispatch))
- **per-contig-mapping-count-aggregation**: Use when a labelled ntLink PAF mapping file (produced with paf=True) must be summarised into counts and per-contig averages, e.g. to reproduce or validate the mapping statistics of Table 5. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-contig-mapping-count-aggregation))
- **per-contig-read-coverage-quantification**: Use when validating metagenome assemblies by measuring what fraction of contig bases are covered by mapped reads — e.g. to detect poorly covered assembler outputs like the CLC bio Horizon oil-spill assembly (~85% coverage vs ~99% for MeGAMerge). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-contig-read-coverage-quantification))
- **per-cycle**: Use when organizing a consequential change into preparation, implementation, and independent evaluation, or when the user asks for PER. Preserve expectations so the result can revise the understanding that guided the work. ([wycats/ai-plugin](https://github.com/wycats/ai-plugin/tree/HEAD/dist/wycats/skills/per-cycle) · ★19 · 2 copies)
- **per-database-count-tabulation**: Reference for adoptions/usage volumes of a chemical classification tool across named chemical databases (DrugBank, HMDB, ChEBI, PubChem, LIPID MAPS) — e.g. building an impact or adoption table for a classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-database-count-tabulation))
- **per-dataset-entry-tabulation**: Use when reconstructing the provenance composition of COCONUT (or a similarly aggregated NP database) from published per-source counts and validating aggregated totals against reported flat/stereo release numbers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-dataset-entry-tabulation))
- **per-dataset-malignant-fraction-aggregation**: Use when you have per-cell malignant-cell predictions from a scRNA-seq annotation model across many datasets and need to compare dataset-level malignant fractions against reference percentages (e.g., CancerSCEM database values). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-dataset-malignant-fraction-aggregation))
- **per-dataset-metrics-table-assembly**: Use when evaluating a pairwise retention-order model over many independent RPLC datasets and you must compute per-dataset accuracy, Spearman, and rank score for model vs. fingerprint baseline, then assemble into a comparison table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-dataset-metrics-table-assembly))
- **per-dataset-summary-table-assembly**: Use when you have run parameter tuning plus feature extraction on multiple LC-MS datasets (different sample types, acquisition modes, or MS vendors) and need one consolidated table recording each dataset's metadata and extracted-feature count to judge robustness across conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-dataset-summary-table-assembly))
- **per-diem**: Look up per diem rates for meals and incidentals by city or country. Use when anyone asks "what's the per diem for [location]", "meal budget", "M&IE rates", "food costs", "daily allowance", "per diem rates", "how much should we budget for food", or any question about meal and incidental expenses for crew travel. ([Beautiful-Destinations/bd-travel-agent](https://github.com/Beautiful-Destinations/bd-travel-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/per-diem))
- **per-diem-calculation-wrong-for-partial-9e75fb1b2a022db4**: Fixes per diem errors on partial travel days by applying the company's first-day/last-day rule to the trip timestamps. Use when the per diem total looks wrong because travel started or ended mid-day. Not for missing rates or missing timestamps. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/per-diem-calculation-wrong-for-partial-9e75fb1b2a022db4))
- **per-diem-claim-failed-travel-dates-don-6fc61b5f456624f1**: Fixes a per-diem claim rejected for date mismatch by aligning the claim dates with the actual travel itinerary. Use when a per-diem claim fails because the travel dates do not match the itinerary. Not for rate-table problems. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/per-diem-claim-failed-travel-dates-don-6fc61b5f456624f1))
- **per-domain-accuracy-aggregation**: Use when you have per-question model predictions and reference answers for a multi-domain benchmark (e.g., CRAG's Finance, Sports, Music, Movie, Open domains) and need to compute and compare accuracy (%) per domain and overall across model conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/per-domain-accuracy-aggregation))
- **per-donor-per-celltype-centroid-computation**: Use when precomputing scTab data augmentation: per-cell-type, per-donor mean expression centroids must be derived from a size-factor-normalized, log1p CxG matrix to build donor-difference augmentation vectors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-donor-per-celltype-centroid-computation))
- **per-droplet-isoform-and-gene-quantification**: Use when you have barcoded long-read alignments to a reference genome and need per-cell/per-droplet gene and isoform count matrices, including novel isoforms, with explicit handling of multimapping reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-droplet-isoform-and-gene-quantification))
- **per-ecosystem-sync-islands**: Use when adding sync of user state (favorites, progress, playlists, preferences) across devices, when adding sign-in, or when tempted to stand up a sync backend. Carries the sync-islands architecture (CloudKit private DB for Apple, Google Drive App Data for Android+Web — the user's own cloud, no server to run), the query-free CloudKit record pattern that replaced a never-worked CKQuery design, tombstones + last-writer-wins merge, optional sign-in gating only sync, and user-visible sync status. Also the web bridging both islands (CloudKit JS + Drive, one merge, tombstones both ways, per-enviro… ([SattikBhaumik/Minerva-University-AI-Research-Team-ATLAS-App](https://github.com/SattikBhaumik/Minerva-University-AI-Research-Team-ATLAS-App/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/per-ecosystem-sync-islands) · 3 copies)
- **per-embryo-cost-normalization**: Use when two-pass imaging timing data (per-slide acquisition time, detected-object counts, full-slide image counts) must be converted into per-sample cost and a speedup factor to benchmark against a reported figure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-embryo-cost-normalization))
- **per-entity-dashboard**: Render a close-package JSON (controller_cycle.py --out-json) into a self-contained, CSP-safe, theme-aware per-entity dashboard — headline KPIs (revenue, net income, gross margin, current ratio, DSO), IS/BS summaries, reconciliation exceptions, top flux, and close-state + traceability/self-certified badges. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-mz-position-fragment-classifier-training))
- **per-node-classifier-selection-svm**: Use when constructing an scHPL hierarchical tree over aligned single-cell datasets and you must decide which classifier (linear SVM, kNN, or one-class SVM) to train per node, especially when unknown/new cell populations must be detectable via rejection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-node-classifier-selection-svm))
- **per-nucleotide-error-profiling**: Use when nanopore reads (cDNA SQK-PCS111 or direct RNA SQK-RNA002) have been aligned to a plasmid or mRNA reference and you need per-position mismatch/insertion/deletion counts or per-read mean error rates against the reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-nucleotide-error-profiling))
- **per-nucleotide-error-statistics-interpretation**: Use when direct RNA nanopore reads aligned to a reference transcript (sorted/indexed BAM) must be scanned for position-specific error enrichment — e.g. elevated mismatch/deletion rates marking N1-methylpseudouridine sites in mRNA vaccines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-nucleotide-error-statistics-interpretation))
- **per-nucleotide-folding-pattern-plotting**: Use when analyzing CLASH/qCLASH hybrid RNA data in 'hyb' format that has a paired Vienna (or CT) predicted-structure file, and you need predicted miRNA/target folding patterns, per-nucleotide pairing, or binding-energy distributions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-nucleotide-folding-pattern-plotting))
- **per-nucleotide-motif-frequency-profiling**: Use when telomeric long reads have already been chromosome-end-assigned (e.g., Chr 7q in sample.telomap.tsv) and the goal is to profile, cluster, and compare sample-specific TVS distribution signatures across the telomeric repeat region. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-nucleotide-motif-frequency-profiling))
- **per-nucleotide-shannon-entropy-mapping**: Use when you have a per-nucleotide Shannon entropy list (two-column, tab-delimited, no header) and a pre-computed RNA secondary structure in CT or dot-bracket format, and need sliding-window structure-count maps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-nucleotide-shannon-entropy-mapping))
- **per-object-depth-estimation-eval**: Evaluates the accuracy of per-object depth estimation for vehicles in autonomous driving scenarios. It measures how well a model predicts the depth of individual objects given monocular RGB images and 2D bounding boxes. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Waymo Open Dataset, KITTI Detection Dataset, KITTI MOT Dataset, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports delta&lt;1.25. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/per-object-depth-estimation-eval) · ★3)
- **per-ontology-citings**: Generate a citings.md file for one pr4xis ontology directory by extracting source-paper citation comments from the surrounding code. Produces a structured bibliography with full citations, DOIs/URLs, and one-line annotations of which axioms each source grounds. Where the code references a paper without a full bibliography entry, scaffold a placeholder for human filling. ([i-am-logger/pr4xis](https://github.com/i-am-logger/pr4xis/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/per-ontology-citings) · ★17)
- **per-ontology-mermaid-external**: Generate a mermaid diagram showing one pr4xis ontology's external connections — the functors and adjunctions that link it to other ontologies in the workspace. Inserts the diagram into the per-ontology README between auto-generated markers. ([i-am-logger/pr4xis](https://github.com/i-am-logger/pr4xis/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/per-ontology-mermaid-external) · ★17)
- **per-ontology-mermaid-internal**: Generate a mermaid diagram showing the internal structure of one pr4xis ontology — its concepts (entities) as nodes and its relationships (morphisms) as edges, with arrow styles distinguishing taxonomy / mereology / causation / opposition. Inserts the diagram into the per-ontology README between auto-generated markers. ([i-am-logger/pr4xis](https://github.com/i-am-logger/pr4xis/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/per-ontology-mermaid-internal) · ★17)
- **per-ontology-readme**: Generate a README.md for one pr4xis ontology directory by reading its ontology.rs (the define_ontology! block), surrounding code, and source-code citation comments. Produces a short, accurate README that orients a new reader. Does not modify ontology.rs itself; only writes the README. ([i-am-logger/pr4xis](https://github.com/i-am-logger/pr4xis/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/per-ontology-readme) · ★17)
- **per-ontology-rollout**: Wrapper that runs the four per-ontology generation skills in sequence on a single ontology directory — README, citings, internal mermaid, external mermaid. Use this for the ([i-am-logger/pr4xis](https://github.com/i-am-logger/pr4xis/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/per-ontology-rollout) · ★17)
- **per-option-value**: Generate ESO (Employee Stock Option) valuation reports. Use this skill when the user needs to value employee stock options, calculate per-option values, or generate ESO valuation reports. ([Vinch1/FinancialAgent](https://github.com/Vinch1/FinancialAgent/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/per-option-value) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **per-order-rv-weighted-average-combination**: Use when deriving an RV time series from echelle spectra (e.g., HARPS S2D) with the classical template-matching formulation, or when establishing a per-order baseline to diagnose whether a semi-Bayesian approach is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/per-order-rv-weighted-average-combination))
- **per-organism-likelihood-ratio-source-assignment**: Use when dZ-positive reads in a mixed metagenome must be traced to their organism of origin, i.e. a per-read Z-Calling SVM TSV exists and reads are aligned to a multi-organism reference and you need per-species LR+ evidence of dZ-DNA presence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-organism-likelihood-ratio-source-assignment))
- **per-patient-fusion-detection-rate-analysis**: Use when a targeted fusion search has produced per-cell fusion calls across a multi-patient clinical cohort and you need per-patient recovery rates and cohort-level sensitivity to validate the calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-patient-fusion-detection-rate-analysis))
- **per-patient-summary-statistics-computation**: Use when you have per-read telomap TSV output for multiple barcoded patient PBL samples and need read-to-patient aggregation (mean telomere length, mean raw telomere length) followed by a Pearson correlation of those summaries against patient age. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-patient-summary-statistics-computation))
- **per-patient-virus-detection-tabulation**: Use when scPathoQuant has been run with a multi-virus reference fasta (up to 2 entries) on 10x cellranger count outputs and you must tabulate which viruses were detected per patient, with per-virus infected-cell counts and read abundances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-patient-virus-detection-tabulation))
- **per-peak-flag-table-export**: Use when you have an mzML file with MS2 fragmentation events and a MZmine2-exported picked-peak list with RT and m/z min/max, and need per-peak DDA coverage flags. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-peak-flag-table-export))
- **per-persona-documentation**: Rewrite documentation or explain code for one named reader at their actual level, so a project manager and a frontend engineer each get the view they need instead of both reading everything, optionally with a short comprehension quiz. Use when documentation is too long or pitched wrong, when someone needs code explained for a specific audience, or when onboarding people from different backgrounds to the same system. ([michellelynne/software-engineers-library](https://github.com/michellelynne/software-engineers-library/tree/HEAD/skills/per-persona-documentation) · 2 copies)
- **per-pixel-array-arithmetic**: Use when you have multiple registered LA-ICP-MS elemental images (e.g., P31, Zn66 intensity maps) and need to compute a new derived image by applying per-pixel operations—such as elemental ratios, thresholding, masking, or conditional logic—across channels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/per-pixel-array-arithmetic))
- **per-pixel-fit-quality-mapping**: Use when a hyperspectral host model cube has been generated from broadband photometry (e.g. PS1 grizy via CIGALE) and you need to verify per-pixel fit quality before projecting the model into SEDm observation space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/per-pixel-fit-quality-mapping))
- **per-plate-group-distribution-verification**: Use when a stratified inter-batch balancing run (e.g., across gender, treatment/placebo, and age-group dimensions) has produced a plate-assignment table and you must confirm each plate's group proportions match the overall cohort ratio before exporting worksheets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-plate-group-distribution-verification))
- **per-plate-linear-model-swapping-fraction-estimation**: Use when quantifying sample-mislabelling (barcode swapping) rates in plate-based scRNA-seq libraries where the same or paired libraries were sequenced on both a non-patterned (HiSeq 2500) and a patterned flow-cell machine (HiSeq 4000/X/NovaSeq). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-plate-linear-model-swapping-fraction-estimation))
- **per-point-signal-difference-computation**: Use when you need to verify that MC smoothing preserves DSF melting-curve shape by quantifying point-wise raw-vs-smoothed residuals on His-FGF7 + 50 µM heparin exemplar data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/per-point-signal-difference-computation))
- **per-position-base-insertion-count-tallying**: Use when consensus sequences must be derived directly from read alignments (SAM/BAM/CRAM, possibly unsorted or streamed from a mapper) and you need fast, memory-efficient per-position base and insertion tallies rather than sorted pileups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-position-base-insertion-count-tallying))
- **per-position-coverage-tabulation**: Use when you have MAPQ/flag-filtered read alignments (BAM) and need per-base counts of soft/hard-clipped reads alongside total coverage to locate candidate assembly-error breakpoints or heterozygous positions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-position-coverage-tabulation))
- **per-position-indel-error-rate-quantification**: Use when aligned PacBio HiFi amplicon reads show an unexplained secondary peak in the edit-distance distribution and you need to test whether the excess errors are insertion-specific and position-dependent (e.g., rising near one alignment position) rather than uniformly distributed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/per-position-indel-error-rate-quantification))
- **per-position-methylation-accuracy-benchmarking**: Use when ONT eventalign TSVs have been classified with trained mCaller models and you must quantify per-position detection accuracy against orthogonal methylated/unmethylated position labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-position-methylation-accuracy-benchmarking))
- **per-position-nucleotide-counting**: Use when MinION reads have been mapped to target sequences (e.g., via LAST) and a per-target consensus in FASTA format is needed, with explicit coverage and ambiguity handling per position. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-position-nucleotide-counting))
- **per-position-nucleotide-diversity-calculation**: Use when you have a sorted .bam of reads mapped to a reference .fasta and need per-position nucleotide diversity plus scaffold-level coverage, breadth, and expected-breadth metrics for microdiversity profiling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/per-position-nucleotide-diversity-calculation))

- **per-project-devtools**: This skill should be used when a project needs language-specific tooling configured (LSP, formatter, linter). - **per-protein-pearson-correlation-evaluation**: Use when a reference-mapping workflow predicts per-cell protein or other modal annotations from mRNA-only query cells and you need a quantitative per-feature accuracy metric against measured values. - **per-protein-summary-statistics-across-droplet-populations**: Use when you have a CITE-seq ADT count matrix split into empty (background) and cell-containing droplets and need to quantify or validate that per-protein ambient background levels are shared between the two populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-protein-summary-statistics-across-droplet-populations))
- **per-query-composite-score-analysis**: Use when you have paired per-query quality scores (e.g. composite scores) from two RAG/IR system configurations over the same query set and must test whether a configuration change (model scale or retrieval component) yields a statistically significant gain. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/per-query-composite-score-analysis))
- **per-question-type-accuracy-reporting**: Use when comparing reported accuracies across question types or datasets (e.g. Mintaka, 2WikiMH, WebQSP) and baseline systems whose metrics are not directly comparable (accuracy vs Hit/F1, differing dataset sizes). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/per-question-type-accuracy-reporting))
- **per-rank-accuracy-tabulation**: Use when a taxonomic classifier's predictions on the 69,335-sequence Greengenes2 2024.09 test set need per-rank top-1 accuracy (and optionally F1/ECE) tabulated, including Seen/Unseen or similarity-bucket subsets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/per-rank-accuracy-tabulation))
- **per-rank-classification-metrics-evaluation**: Use when DeepTaxa (or comparable hierarchical 16S rRNA classifier) predictions on labeled test sequences must be scored per taxonomic rank, e.g. reproducing the Greengenes2 2024.09 species-level accuracy/F1/ECE benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/per-rank-classification-metrics-evaluation))
- **per-reaction-fraction-computation**: Use when metabolite annotations are tagged with BioTransformer reaction types and you need detected-vs-predicted counts and detection fractions per biotransformation (oxidation, glucuronidation, etc.). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-reaction-fraction-computation))
- **per-read-annotation-generation**: Use when a long-read metagenomics pipeline must flag ARG-carrying reads and emit per-read records (hit coordinates, plasmid status, lineage) that feed Argo's read-overlap clustering and host-assignment steps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-read-annotation-generation))
- **per-read-error-profile-analysis**: Use when benchmarking nanopore signal simulators or basecallers on DNA/RNA reads: you have POD5/FASTQ read sets plus a reference and need per-read accuracy, Q-score, error-type breakdowns, and DTW signal distances aggregated to medians. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-read-error-profile-analysis))
- **per-read-event-alignment-to-reference**: Use when tombo-resquiggled single fast5 files for a MODIFIED or UNMODIFIED direct RNA nanopore sample must be stacked into a 2D (num_reads x target_rna_length) signal array for per-read modification analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-read-event-alignment-to-reference))
- **per-read-mapping-agreement-assessment**: Use when comparing mapping outputs from two different salmon versions or implementations (e.g., C++ 1.12.0 vs. Rust 2.0) to determine if observed differences in total mapped read counts are due to true algorithmic bugs, parameter defaults, or index format changes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/per-read-mapping-agreement-assessment))
- **per-read-mapping-quality-analysis**: Use when you have a per-read feature table from raw nanopore signal mapping (read_id, mapping-quality ratio, chain-score ratio, mean chain-score ratio) and need to classify reads as mapped or unmapped with a weighted threshold rule. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-read-mapping-quality-analysis))
- **per-read-methylation-aggregation**: Use when you have CHEUI Model 1 read-level m6A/m5C predictions from pooled nanopore direct RNA replicates and need to define significantly modified sites, filter transcript/site coverage, and compute single-molecule modification statistics such as co-occurrence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-read-methylation-aggregation))
- **per-read-methylation-calling**: Use when bisulfite-treated reads have been aligned with the 'three-letter' approach and you need per-read/per-site methylation states recovered by comparing alignments back to the original (un-converted) reference genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/per-read-methylation-calling))
- **per-read-methylation-f1-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking per-read 5mC methylation calling accuracy of ONT callers (DeepMod2, Guppy, Dorado) on HG002/HG003/HG004 data against short-read bisulfite ground truth (GSE186383). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-read-methylation-f1-evaluation))
- **per-read-methylation-score-evaluation**: Use when you have per-read methylation scores (TSV with ID/Chr/Pos/Strand/Score columns) from one or more nanopore 5mC-calling tools and control reads of known methylation status (0%/100% sets), and need to quantify per-read detection accuracy via ROC/AUC and precision-recall curves. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-read-methylation-score-evaluation))
- **per-read-modification-aggregation-to-genome-space**: Use when NanoSpeech basecalled fasta/q read headers carry per-modification base indexes and you need per-read BED plus genome-space aggregated modification calls (counts, depth, frequency) for a reference transcriptome or genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-read-modification-aggregation-to-genome-space))
- **per-read-modification-coordinate-extraction-from-bam**: Use when NanoSpeech basecalled dRNA pod5/fast5 reads into fasta/fastq whose headers embed indexes of candidate modified nucleotides (e.g. inosines), and you need per-read and transcriptome-space modification coordinates to score calls against known modification positions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-read-modification-coordinate-extraction-from-bam))
- **per-read-probability-aggregation-stoichiometry**: Use when nanopore direct RNA sequencing reads have been classified per-read by CHEUI-solo Model 1 and transcriptome-wide site-level methylation probabilities and stoichiometry estimates are needed for individual experimental conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-read-probability-aggregation-stoichiometry))
- **per-read-score-consensus-modeling**: Use when per-read methylation score files (ID, Chr, Pos, Strand, Score) from multiple nanopore methylation callers are available and single-tool predictions show poor agreement with expected methylation frequencies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-read-score-consensus-modeling))
- **per-read-score-file-formatting**: Use when per-read methylation predictions from multiple nanopore methylation callers must be merged into a common format before training or applying METEORE's random forest or multiple linear regression combination models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-read-score-file-formatting))
- **per-read-tail-table-generation**: Use when PAIso-seq clean CCS reads have been aligned with minimap2 -x splice to mm10/GRCm38 and per-read poly(A) tail lengths must be called from terminal clipped sequences under explicit length/purity thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-read-tail-table-generation))
- **per-read-taxonomic-assignment-aggregation**: Use when per-read taxonomic assignment files (e.g. MetaMaps reads2Taxon/WIMP output, or Centrifuge/Bracken/MEGAN-LR classifications) on simulated i100, p25, CAMI, HMP7, or Zymo long-read datasets must be turned into composition profiles and validated via Pearson r2 and L1 distance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/per-read-taxonomic-assignment-aggregation))
- **per-read-taxonomic-benchmarking**: Use when comparing taxonomic classifiers for host identification of ARG-carrying long reads, or when quantifying misclassification rates (true-positive, false-positive, unclassified) of metagenomic profilers against known ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-read-taxonomic-benchmarking))
- **per-read-telomap-table-filtering**: Use when you have a telomap main output table (sample.telomap.tsv) and need to extract per-read telomeric regions for specific barcodes or chromosome ends to compute raw vs canonical telomere lengths or motif binding-site densities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-read-telomap-table-filtering))
- **per-record-annotation-quality-reporting**: Use when an annotated .msp spectral library must be checked per record that SMILES, InChI, InChIKey, or CAS number metadata are well-formed before export or downstream use. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/per-reference-assembly-metric-normalization))
- **per-region-count-tabulation**: Use when you have variant-calling results (e.g. VCF-style call sets from two simulators on R10.4. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-region-count-tabulation))
- **per-replicon-metric-normalization**: Use when benchmarking metagenomic de novo assemblers against a defined mock community and you need a per-reference-replicon completeness fraction (LSA) from contig-to-reference alignments. - **per-repo-conventions**: Use when starting work in any repository, and again at each work-phase transition (design, coding, debugging, verification, opening a PR). ([microsoft/amplifier-foundation](https://github.com/microsoft/amplifier-foundation/tree/HEAD/skills/per-repo-conventions) · ★17)

- **per-root-metabolite-profiling**: Use when when you have deposited mass spectrometry imaging datasets for plant roots in CDF format paired with pre-computed Matlab workspaces, and you need to reproduce per-root linear-axis metabolite profiling outputs to validate reported genotype-level results (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/per-root-metabolite-profiling) · 2 copies)
- **per-sample-aberration-classification**: Use when SCISSOR shape-change calls (per-sample GSC/LSC sample IDs) must be validated against an external mutation repository such as a TCGA MAF to label each call as a known or previously unrecognized aberration. - **per-sample-count-matrix-merging**: Use when per-sample RiboSeq alignments (BAM) exist and a single merged count matrix, one column per sample indexed by transcript/gene ID, is needed as input to DESeq2 differential expression analysis. - **per-sample-depth-calibration**: Use when constructing simulated shotgun metagenomic benchmark datasets where each sample must hit a target mean depth, and per-sample read counts must be computed from depth, reference size, and read length. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/per-sample-depth-calibration))
- **per-sample-dmdt-table-parsing**: Use when a task requires computing the fraction of disease samples exhibiting a specific isoform switch (e.g. HSPH1-001 vs HSPH1-002) from CanIsoNet's per-sample dMDT tables with expression values, rather than gene-level summary statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-sample-dmdt-table-parsing))
- **per-sample-feature-composition-analysis**: Use when when you have aligned and quantified mass spectrometry features from multiple natural extracts (via MZmine2/3), paired with in silico annotation results (ISDB or SIRIUS), and you need to prioritize samples for chemical discovery based on the proportion of unannotated, extract-specific. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/per-sample-feature-composition-analysis) · 2 copies)
- **per-sample-gene-count-aggregation**: Use when shotgun metagenomic FASTQ reads must be converted into a sample-by-gene count/frequency matrix via alignment against a non-redundant gene catalog, prior to CSS normalization or KEGG-based functional annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/per-sample-gene-count-aggregation))
- **per-sample-gene-richness-quantification**: Use when you need per-sample, per-species gene counts from vaginal metagenomic or metatranscriptomic FASTQ data to assess intraspecies gene-content diversity, such as testing whether multiple strains of a species co-occur in a community. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/per-sample-gene-richness-quantification))
- **per-sample-kreport2-report-generation**: Use when running real-time or batch Oxford Nanopore metagenomic analysis with Nanometa Live and you need per-barcode Kraken2 classification reports in results/kraken2/ that the GUI and load_kraken_data consume. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-sample-kreport2-report-generation))
- **per-sample-outlier-detection**: Use when per-cell SenePy hub scores must be summarized per biological sample and tested between disease groups, or when individual samples may distort group-level senescence comparisons. - **per-sample-pass-fail-classification**: Use when after LC-MS data acquisition is complete (or during real-time monitoring) and you have loaded processed LC-MS data in mzML or vendor format and defined QC criteria (retention time windows, m/z tolerances, intensity thresholds) for your internal standards and target analytes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/per-sample-pass-fail-classification) · 2 copies)
- **per-sample-read-count-matrix-merging**: Use when COMPSRA annotation has produced per-sample read-count files for multiple FASTQ inputs and you need a single merged RNA-molecule × sample count matrix (e.g., 12 serum samples × 6 RNA classes). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-sample-read-count-matrix-merging))
- **per-sample-read-count-tabulation**: Use when you have MiniBar-demultiplexed Nanopore FASTA/FASTQ records with sample IDs in header comments and need per-specimen read tallies, e.g. to quantify assignment yield across barcode edit-distance settings (-e 2/3/4). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-sample-read-count-tabulation))
- **per-sample-rmse-benchmark-comparison**: Use when a new metabolite identification/quantification method's output reconstructions must be validated against an established tool on identical 1H NMR spectra, to demonstrate lower per-sample reconstruction error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-sample-rmse-benchmark-comparison))
- **per-sample-spectral-aggregation**: Use when you have raw MS2 spectra from a sample and need to collapse them into a single sample-level representation for comparison across multiple samples, particularly when samples have poor feature overlap, strong retention time shifts between LC methods, or were acquired on different mass. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/per-sample-spectral-aggregation) · 3 copies)
- **per-sample-variant-count-benchmarking**: Use when reproducing or validating a published WGS/WES pipeline's variant yield: public SRA FASTQ samples are given with accession IDs (PRJNA/SRR) and expected total variant counts per project to compare against. - **per-seat-per-core-usage-metrics**: Für Seat/Core/Usage-Metriken: ordnet Norm, Beweislast und Gegenargument; Ergebnis: Prüfprodukt mit Risiko und nächstem Schritt. ([vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim](https://github.com/vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim/tree/HEAD/softwarerecht-de-eu-us/skills/per-seat-per-core-usage-metrics))
- **per-section-tracking**: Breaks down weekly and trailing-4-week substacker performance per Substack section, keyed on section tags in corpus/published/ and section-map.md. Reports opens, clicks, subs-attributable-to-section per section with ≥3 posts. Skips if section-map has &lt;2 sections. Feeds Curator with pruning candidates (sections with 4-week median z ≤ -1.0). Use when section-map has ≥2 live sections. Trigger keywords — per-section, section performance, section metrics, section pruning, differential engagement. ([lyndonkl/claude](https://github.com/lyndonkl/claude/tree/HEAD/skills/per-section-tracking) · ★200)

Invoke explicitly to document a specific simulation, such as Wa-Tor, Conway, Forest-fire, Langton, Sugarscape, Snake, ET Pets, or Rebounding. ([mkalb/etpetssim](https://github.com/mkalb/etpetssim/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/per-simulation-docs) · ★2)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-site-methylation-frequency-aggregation))
- **per-site-methylation-frequency-binarization**: Use when preparing per-read (output.per_read) or per-site (output.per_site) methylation predictions for binarized benchmarking against discrete bisulfite ground-truth labels. - **per-site-read-feature-aggregation**: Use when base-called ONT direct RNA reads must be condensed into per-site error/quality features over RRACH motif adenosines to score RNA modifications at site level, since per-read prediction is not yet supported. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/per-site-read-feature-aggregation))
- **per-source-sensitivity-analysis**: Use when benchmarking a metagenomic fecal source tracking tool against mesocosms of known composition and you need per-source accuracy, e.g. to detect under-performing sources like cow. - **per-spectrum-runtime-measurement**: Use when evaluating or comparing FSA formula-ranking throughput across reference-list scopes or datasets, where rank#1/top-5 accuracy must be reported alongside an average seconds-per-spectrum runtime metric. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-spectrum-runtime-measurement))

- **per-spot-module-expression-scoring**: Use when you have a merged, edge-pruned SCING GRN divided into leiden subnetworks and need quantitative module activity scores per spatial spot or cell to support regional-specificity tests, clustering, or phenotype/plaque correlation analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-stage-expression-index-aggregation))
- **per-status-f1-variance-analysis**: Use when comparing microbiome classification models on multi-class datasets (e.g. 3-class oral or 5-class gut status labels) and the question is whether per-class performance is balanced, not just whether overall Kappa or accuracy is higher. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/per-status-f1-variance-analysis))
- **per-step-credit-assignment-evaluation**: Use when you need to compare reward model supervision granularities: you have math-problem completions with final-answer labels and/or step-level labels, and must show whether a reward model can score intermediate reasoning steps versus only final outcomes. - **per-store-fan-out**: Fan out multi-store, multi-repository or multi-channel investigations with read-only inspection sub-agents, shared resolved inputs, bounded iteration and auditable manifests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-subclass-annotation-stratification))
- **per-superclass-statistical-aggregation**: Use when you have per-compound cosine-similarity scores from spectral prediction evaluation and need to check whether prediction quality is uniform across compound classes rather than only reporting a global median. - **per-task-benchmark-accuracy-aggregation**: Use when comparing two or more celltyping methods evaluated on the same set of matched tasks (e.g., 50 Cellcano benchmark tasks) and you need a statistical test of whether one method's accuracy advantage is significant. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/per-task-benchmark-accuracy-aggregation))

- **per-task-review-loop**: Inline implementation quality gate that fills the gap /auto-flow and /delegate don't cover — serial single-session work where each task could break independently and parallel-dispatch isn't applicable. Two modes: (a) per-task — after each implementation task dispatch a stateless reviewer in background while you continue; PASS log+continue, FAIL pause+fix+re-dispatch, IMPROVE apply if cheap. (b) plan-chain — before locking a plan, run 4 reviewers in series (P1 stateless → P2 comparative → P3 cairn-grounded → P4 coherence) so each builds on the prior verdict. Make sure to invoke this skill when… ([ziyilam3999/three-role-model](https://github.com/ziyilam3999/three-role-model/tree/HEAD/skills/per-task-review-loop) · ★1)
- **per-taxon-accuracy-benchmarking**: Use when benchmarking trajectory-reconstruction accuracy of denoised relative abundances per taxon across samples, e.g. comparing LUMINATE against TGP-CODA/MALLARD on simulated longitudinal count data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/per-taxon-accuracy-benchmarking))
- **per-taxon-probe-count-balancing**: Use when building a hybridization-capture probe set from HUBDesign CandidateBaitRegions.tab and you need the final probe count capped and distributed fairly across all input taxa, e.g. tiling to 5x density with at most 13,500 baits. - **per-tenant-database**: Multi-tenant data isolation with one Rivet Actor per tenant: the actor key is the tenant id, so each tenant gets its own isolated dataset and migrations. ([rivet-dev/skills](https://github.com/rivet-dev/skills) · ★53 · 1.1k installs)
- **per-tenant-database**: Multi-tenant data isolation with one Rivet Actor per tenant: the actor key is the tenant id, so each tenant gets its own isolated dataset and migrations. ([adzamkomladev/lindis-store](https://github.com/adzamkomladev/lindis-store/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/per-tenant-database))
- **per-tissue-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when validating a pre-trained malignant-cell classifier (e.g. Cancer-Finder checkpoints/sc_pretrain_article.pkl) against per-cell malignancy labels from TISCH across multiple tissues, needing per-tissue and weighted-average accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-tissue-accuracy-evaluation))
- **per-tissue-allele-ratio-comparison**: Use when ASE variant calls are available for multiple tissues and you need to quantify reference-allele mapping bias, or to validate that masking a reference genome with called variants reduces inflated reference allele ratios toward 50%. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-tissue-allele-ratio-comparison))
- **per-tissue-ase-variant-counting**: Use when you have per-tissue filtered biallelic SNP VCFs and need to quantify how many SNPs show allele-specific expression and what fraction of informative SNPs this represents. - **per-tissue-cross-validation-evaluation**: Use when labeled nc-sindels (cis-eQTL positives vs negatives) must be classified per GTEx tissue and model comparison requires within-tissue AUROC/AUPRC medians across 44 tissues rather than a pooled single-split evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/per-tissue-cross-validation-evaluation))
- **per-tissue-informative-snp-identification**: Use when you have per-tissue RNA-seq VCF files (e.g., GATK HaplotypeCaller g.vcf-derived) and need to count or extract biallelic SNPs eligible for binomial-test ASE analysis before running the ASE testing stage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/per-tissue-informative-snp-identification))
- **per-tool-installation-status-tracking**: Use when installing the MetumpX toolset on Ubuntu 18.04 and you need to capture, per selected tool, whether dependency resolution and installation succeeded or failed from the installer's on-screen dialogue and logs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-tool-installation-status-tracking))
- **per-tool-percentage-tabulation**: Use when comparing tool-generated HGVS annotations (HGVSc/HGVSp) against a reference standard such as ClinVar and you need to quantify, per tool and per field, what fraction of mismatches are missing values, position/variant-type shifts, or non-preferred syntax. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-tool-percentage-tabulation))
- **per-tool-performance-tabulation**: Use when comparing in silico metabolism predictors on a common test set (e.g., EBoMD2, 60Reactants.sdf, or BioTransformer's hold-out SDF) and you need per-tool, per-enzyme Jaccard matrices to compute relative improvement margins. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/per-tool-performance-tabulation))
- **per-tool-reproducibility-benchmarking**: Use when the same biological samples have been sequenced in two independent runs and you need to quantify how consistently competing rRNA classification tools assign OTUs and report correlated abundances across runs.
